# STUDY_OCT10_files: every file each reader described (path, purpose, key results)

## research-root

- `/home/user/v2pons/research/REPORT_24.54.md`: Report section 24.54 of the owner's earlier research series: runs report 24.45's pending first-seat test on operator A's v4 launches from chain data instead of live money, prices the first seat, derives the sub-1% follow rule, lists the other launchers in the 1.5-5% band and summarises the four-conditions survey. Cites src/analysis/v4_slowrug_pull.py and data/derived/edge_check/Q/ (the old repository's paths; here research/ and research/data/). Key results: One launcher 0x3194e326: 107-126 launches/day Sep 27-29, 22-56/day from Sep 30 at 1.2-5% fees, 0.01-0.5% fees from Oct 5 13:00 (54, 133, 111 on Oct 5-7; 13 by Oct 8 10:30), 2-2.45% tier stopped (0 on Oct 6-8). 99% of swaps on 602 launches are its own (~2,000 single-use + ~50 wash wallets, 5-11 swaps per launch). Crowd first buy at block 22/44/64, 34 buys and 1.5 ETH in 30 s; 94% pulled within 20 min (median block 2,393 = 4 min). First seat: 141 of 602 drew a real outside buy, 8 before the crowd, the one clean case (Sep 30, $2.54 at +1, 1.35% fee) got no crowd and a 100% pull 14 blocks later; with 24.45's two early buyers 3 of 3 first-seat outsiders got no pump. Reflex: 2%+ tiers pull 5 blocks after an outside buy ($2.6-5: 65% within 1 s, 74% within 3 s, n 23; $5-13: 88%/94%, n 16); sub-1% tier 10-19 blocks (mode 15), $2.6-5 44% within 3 s n 16, $5-13 100% n 10; first outside buy -> pull within 5 s on 71% of 141 vs placebo 1.6%. Outsiders: 183 positions, 164 lost everything, -3.68 ETH on 4.41 ETH in. $3 seat on paper +22%/+55%/+82% at 10/20/30 s, ~90% win; with the reflex at most -60% and -13% a trade. The follow rule (sub-1% tier): buy 2 blocks after the first real-size buy (crowd on 306 of 312), sell 4 blocks later +3.6% (83% win, p5 -0.8%), 9 blocks later +6.5% (94%), same at $3 and $25, positive every day Oct 5-8 (+2.4% to +6.2%); 553 of 560 non-dust buys inside the hold are the operator's; 2% tiers -0.2% to +0.3%. Other launchers: 0x815542e8 (3%, 179 launches) no crowd, seat -6.6%, outsiders -28%; 0x58daec31 ('a multicall many launchers share' = the PositionManager) outsiders lost 37 ETH on 45; 0x7c7e48f9 (2%) seat about -5%. Survey verdict: the four conditions hold together only for Pons on Robinhood Chain; Arbitrum One left FCFS Sep 24; Clanker v4 and Bankr/Doppler have time taxes but no crowd. Watch items: Robinhood turning on tip ordering (ArbOS 61), a Virtuals v5 router on Robinhood, a time-tax launchpad on a FCFS chain. States a live test would need the feed-read launch, the feed-read crowd buy, a v4 buy-and-sell relay (BuyOnce is Pons-only) and a sell 0.4-0.9 s after the buy: 'Not built: research only' (this repository built it the same day).
- `/home/user/v2pons/research/v4_slowrug_pull.py`: Data puller for report 24.54: every v4 Initialize with ETH as currency0 in a time window (block_at binary search), kept if no hook, tick spacing 200 and fee in the 1.5-5% band (FEE_LO 15000, FEE_HI 50000), or, with a launcher address as 4th argument, pools OUTSIDE the band (fees 1%, 883131, 880238 excepted) whose launch transaction calls that launcher; then every Swap and ModifyLiquidity on each pool within HORIZON 12,000 blocks, the sender/to/nonce/value/selector of every transaction behind them (batched eth_getTransactionByHash, 50 per batch), and the launch blocks' timestamps. Writes one uncompressed JSON (keys window, blocks, family, pools, swaps, liquidity, txs, swap_fields, liquidity_fields, tx_fields, launch_ts). Public RPC with a Chrome User-Agent, 429 backoff up to 60 s, eth_getLogs splits on 'exceeds limit' errors, 99,999-block spans for topic-batched queries. Key results: Produced research/data/slowrug.json.gz (band run: 621 pools, 60,174 swaps, 3,542 liquidity events, 61,965 txs) and slowrug_op.json.gz (launcher run: 371 pools, 60,496 swaps, 733 liquidity events, 61,228 txs), window 2026-09-29 21:00 - 2026-10-08 10:30, blocks 75962099-83244647. Its stdout (counts, call totals) was not kept. Outputs are written uncompressed; the repository holds gzipped copies.
- `/home/user/v2pons/research/v4_slowrug_test.py`: Report 24.54's analysis: loads one or more pull files (.json or .json.gz), assigns a role to every swap (dep = launch sender; fresh = nonce <= 2 at first swap; wash = trades only this launcher's launches, 4+ of them, 3+ swaps per launch on average; dust = other buy under 0.0002 ETH; out = established outsider), prices a $3 first seat at the head of b0+1 with float single-range math at holds 50/100/200/300 blocks (-100% if the pull lands first), and prints six sections: supply per day and launcher, anatomy, outsiders before the crowd, the pull reflex by buy size and tier (1 s = 10 blocks, 3 s = 30), what outsiders got, the paper seat per tier/day with the reflex priced, and the follow variant (sub-1% tier, k = 4 and 9 blocks, $3 and $25). Writes <arg1 without .json(.gz)>_rows.json beside the first input. Fetches launch timestamps from the RPC only for pools whose file lacks them (none in the committed files, so it runs offline). Key results: Reproduced here offline from scratchpad copies: output byte-identical to research/data/slowrug_test.txt. Constants: ETHUSD 2570, STAKE $3, GAS $0.02 per round trip, HOLDS (50, 100, 200, 300) blocks. Uses float math (buy/sell helpers), not src/v4math.py.

- `/home/user/v2pons/research/live_trades.py`: Reads a wallet's live trades back from the chain: operator A's launches (Initialize with ETH currency0 whose launch tx is to 0x3194e326) in a time window, every Swap/ModifyLiquidity within 12,000 blocks, our swaps found by tx sender (router route) or Swap sender = our relay; prints (1) each trade with trigger offset, return, pull/sell offsets, buys behind ours, (2) the trade repriced as if sold in each block +1..+7 (v4math.sell_out_int, gas 2 x 110k x 0.0201 gwei), (3) the release-0.3 exit (TP 1% on the first paying buy behind ours, sent by buy+2 = HOLD 4 - LEAD 2, landing +1 or +2 blocks later), (4) the operator's other launches in the window with their pull delay after the first real buy. Imports src/v4math.py via ../src relative to the file. Key results: Committed with release 0.3 (3b359f5). Docstring says 'four wins and three -100% losses' (the output counts 3 positive).

- `/home/user/v2pons/research/data/SURVEY.md`: The four conditions (FCFS with no priority fee; a launch tax keyed to discrete time; an exact curve; a pre-tick crowd visible on the feed) checked chain by chain and launchpad by launchpad (Oct 8, 2026), with the on-chain checks (chain_ordering, doppler, slowrug) and the web sources. Written for the owner's Pons seat (reports 21.3, 24.20, 24.50, not in this repository). Key results: Robinhood Chain: FCFS (ArbOS 61 lets the owner turn tip collection on, ships off), feed wss://feed.mainnet.chain.robinhood.com one message per ~100 ms block with reverts, compliance-voided txs show status 0, paid faster relays exist (BlockRazor, Dwellir): conditions 1 and 4 yes. Arbitrum One: priority-fee ordering since Sep 24, 2026 (PGA in 125 ms rounds, Timeboost retired, paid Fast Feed). Other Orbit chains FCFS by default, feeds unconfirmed; MegaETH executes on arrival, 10 ms mini-blocks, ordering undocumented; ZKsync/Abstract no pre-block feed; Base/OP/Unichain/etc., Solana, BNB, Monad and the rest priority-fee or builder ordered. Launchpads: Pons V2 99%/6.18%/0.19%/0 per whole second (9900 bps, 3 s), buys only, up to 32 creator-named exempt wallets, constant product: all four conditions, the existing trade. Clanker v4 MevDescendingFees: swaps revert in the deployment second then 66.7% -> 4.2% over 15 s, LP fee so sells pay it, 13 launches in 7 days, no crowd. Bankr/LONG Doppler Rehype: linear 80% -> 1.69% over 10 s (steps of ~7.8 points, the second-10 step from 9.5% to 1.69%), 1,135 launches/day, 4% with a first-minute swap. Operator A's family: no tax, a scripted pump, a pull answering outside buys in 0.5-1.5 s: a trap. Zora (Base) 99% -> 1% over 10 s; Virtuals v5 floor(99(D-t)/D)% with D 60 s/10 min/98 min, scheduled start, Robinhood deploy scripts exist but no live router; Clanker SniperAuctionV2 priced on tx.gasprice; Meteora DBC, Jupiter Studio, Heaven (Solana), four.meme X Mode (BSC) all on priority-fee chains. Watch: a tip-ordering switch on Robinhood (getCollectTips), a Virtuals v5 router on Robinhood, a time-tax launchpad on a FCFS Orbit chain or MegaETH.

## ops-ops2-third: research/session_oct9_10/ops, ops2, third (operator A's launcher pulls/disassembly/permission probe, the A/B crowd and B-launch lists, the Oct 8 copy-scheme collector, the money-loop traces, the A-vs-B mixing tests, the Relay.link/Base cash-out follow, and the third (bonding-curve) launch system)

- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/lib.py`: ops helper: imports chain.py, defines D (scratchpad ops/), Transfer/Approval topics, utc(), getblocks(lo,hi) with batch splitting on 'too large', T() tx row, scan(a,b,pred,backwards,stop) full-block scanner, nonce_now(). Key results: library only; hardcoded scratchpad paths
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/scan.py`: Full-block scanner writing idx/<start>.json.gz chunks of 5,000 blocks: every plain ETH transfer (P) and every tx touching/mentioning a watch.txt address (W); threaded. Usage scan.py a b threads. Key results: output dir ops/idx not in repo
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/watch.txt`: 71 watch addresses for scan.py: the 32 A + 31 B crowd wallets plus 8 B deployers/hubs (0x0545c67e, 0x1821144f, 0x516f7e91, 0xa57fb285, 0xa8e0c37d, 0xae35895b, 0xb4d94437, 0xdb1b7d29). Key results: 71 addresses
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/ab_addrs.txt`: 63 addresses = crowd A (32) + crowd B (31), the address file for mlogs.py. Key results: 63 addresses
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/bwallets.txt`: The 31 crowd-B wallets on one line. Key results: 31 addresses
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/b_list.py`: Reproduces the A/B crowd families from protect/load_all (+annotate): B = wallets whose launches are a subset of the 7 launches of 0x4c45af68 (>=3), A = wallets on >=20 launches, fresh = nonce<=3 and <=2 launches, speck = 0xb4d43149; writes b_list.json. Key results: fam: fresh 798, A 32, B 31, speck 1
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/b_list.json`: Crowd lists: A (32 addresses), B (31 with per-wallet launches/buys/sells/eth_in/eth_out/nonce range), B7 (7 B launch pool ids), fam (862 wallets). Key results: B wallets: 3-7 launches each, 5-76 buys, 0.34-2.88 ETH in, nonces 20-2086; e.g. 0x46e6386b 67 buys 2.837 in / 2.209 out
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/b_launches.py`: Per B launch: launch/pull details and swaps aggregated by group (A/B/fresh/outsider/us/dust/speck) before/after the pull; writes b_launches.json. Key results: needs protect/load_all caches
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/b_launches.json`: The 7 B launches with pull block, token, net ETH in before pull, per-group buys/sells, outsider wallets. Key results: 05:59 0xca04a1e3 pull +816 net 0.366; 06:05 0x069688d0 no pull, 232 B buys 4.59 in; 11:02 0x5736fabe +5391 net 4.47 (189 buys 12.39 in); 14:32 0xe27bfddf +9901 net 2.75, us 0.0012 in/0.0011 out; Oct 9 05:57 0xca847f04 +15269 net 2.84; 09:24 0x758c3c5f +2764 net 1.65; 10:48 0x2a928e99 +18096 net 3.61 (396 buys 9.85 in), us 0.0012/0.0012; outsiders <=3 wallets, <=0.058 ETH per launch
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/bcurve.py`: Pulls every log of bonding-curve contract 0xe33e9e47 from START (default 82,000,000) to head into bcurve_factory.json; counts creation topic 0xdcacba5e. Key results: output bcurve_factory.json not in repo
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/bcurve2.py`: Fragment: prints first creation log, counts distinct creators; dead code for the listing topic 0xe4b7e48f. Key results: no output saved; incomplete script
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/bcx.py`: Helpers over the lch/bc block cache: touching(addrs, lo, hi, desc, stop) and show(). Key results: library
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/bprof.py`: Log-based wallet profile (ERC-20/721 Transfer to/from, Approval by) from START to head -> prof/<w>.json. Key results: bprof.out is empty (0 bytes); prof/ not in repo
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/bprof.out`: Output of bprof.py. Key results: empty file
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/code.py`: eth_getCode of an address -> code_<addr10>.hex, prints length and keccak. Key results: produced the two .hex files
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/code_0x3194e326.hex`: Runtime bytecode of the original launcher. Key results: 13,970 hex chars = 6,984 bytes, hash 42544ef5
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/code_0x816d73e4.hex`: Runtime bytecode of the Oct 8 14:59 copy launcher 0x816d73e4. Key results: 6,984 bytes, hash 2793923d; differs from original at char 13,715 onward (metadata only)
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/deployers.py`: For each B launch deployer: funding tx scanned back 40k blocks, every tx sent after the pull -> deployers_fund.json. Key results: output not in repo

- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/dis_launcher.txt`: Disassembly of the original launcher. - `research/session_oct9_10/ops/dis_copy.txt`: Disassembly of copy 0x816d73e4. Key results: 4,505 lines; diff vs launcher is 69 lines, all in the metadata tail (pc 6856-6983)
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/fpcmp.py`: Compare A vs B swap fingerprints (fp keys, via, to, selector), buy offset after launch, buy sizes. Key results: no output saved
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/hop.py`: Funding of a wallet: scan back for a value tx to it. Usage hop.py w B [maxback]. Key results: see hop1.out
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/hop1.out`: hop.py on 0xe2f7e70b (funder of the Oct 8 05:59 B deployer). Key results: 0xc88072d8 -> 0xe2f7e70b 5.726264 ETH (nonce 0, blk 83085782 05:57:40), then 0xe2f7e70b -> 0x0545c67e 1.0 ETH (nonce 0)
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/lchlogs.py`: All logs of launcher 0x3194e326 from block 0 to head in 2M-block queries -> launcher_logs.json. Key results: output (7,485 Launched events) not in repo
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/lchtx.py`: Fetch the tx behind every launcher log -> launcher_txs.json (from, nonce, value, input, gas, type, tip). Key results: output not in repo
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/lists.py`: All bonding-curve listing events (topic 0xe4b7e48f, topic1 = address) naming any A/B crowd wallet, blocks 82,000,000 -> head; joins creator/token from bcurve_factory.json -> lists.json. Key results: needs bcurve_factory.json (missing)
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/lists.json`: 59 bonding-curve tokens whose listing names A or B crowd wallets: curve address -> block, nA, nB, creator, token, txs. Key results: 13 curves list A (30-31 wallets, blocks 83,274,197-83,684,064; creators are the A loop's launch wallets e.g. 0x38dcbde1, 0x9f970621, 0xcfcf219e), 46 list B (4-31 wallets), 0 list both; last four (84,134,665-84,174,827) list 18 B wallets, creator 0xba8e56d0 = the Oct 9 11:51 third-system launch
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/mlogs.py`: Batched multi-address eth_getLogs (Transfer to/from, Approval by) in 30k-block chunks with bisection on failure. Usage mlogs.py START END out.json addrfile. Key results: see mlogs.out
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/mlogs.out`: Progress log of one mlogs.py run (618 jobs = 206 windows x 3 filters). Key results: 50,861 logs collected 12:50-13:07; output json not in repo
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/oct9b.py`: Oct 9 B launches 0x758c3c5f and 0x2a928e99: deployer funding back 3 hops and deployer txs after the pull -> oct9b.json. Key results: crashed (NameError D) per oct9b.out; never produced output
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/oct9b.out`: Traceback of oct9b.py. Key results: NameError: name 'D' is not defined (bcx/sets do not export D)
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/sets.py`: Builds Asys/Bsys address sets from b_list, lists.json creators, loop logs and five tr/ traces; adds collector 0x3fc5b8b8 to Bsys. Key results: module-level side effects; imported by cross.py/oct9b.py
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/speed.py`: RPC speed probe: 100 full blocks timing, historical eth_getBalance at head-100..-100000. Key results: no output saved
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/speed2.py`: Threaded batch-size test of eth_getBlockByNumber (50/100 per batch, 1-4 threads). Key results: no output saved
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/lch/bc.py`: Shared compact block cache (bc/q<c>.json per 250 blocks, legacy 1000-block files) with retrying post(), _getblocks() halving batch size on failure, txs()/txs_desc() iterators. Key results: cache dir ops/lch/bc not in repo; any user re-fetches from RPC
- `research/session_oct9_10/ops/lch/trace.py`: Money graph around a launch: deployer funding back 3 hops (stop at funder nonce>50), funder's fan-out, payout forward 2 hops, collector inputs -> tr/<pid10>.json. Usage trace.py pid-prefix... Key results: 26 traces produced

## explore (research/session_oct9_10/explore: the 33,984-rule search on 24 families, the front rule's exact replay in adv/ and chk/, the v5.0 traps)

## dry1 (research/session_oct9_10/dry1 and dry1/skeptic): the front dry run checked against the chain, the 435-pool census of the 24 h before, the skeptic re-derivation, the live audit of the first 132 front trades, engine_log.txt

- `/home/user/v2pons/research/session_oct9_10/dry1/fetch.py`: RPC fetcher for replay.py: every v2.433 family launch from FROM=84856239 to head-300 (Initialize logs on the PoolManager with currency0 = ETH, chunked getLogs; launch tx must be to the PositionManager 0x58daec31, 1,444 bytes, 2.4333285 ETH; shape fee 2500 / tick spacing 50 / hooks 0), then per pool 36 full blocks b0..b0+35 (eth_getBlockByNumber with txs) and the launch receipt, then every Swap/ModifyLiquidity log of the family to head (40 pids per getLogs), then the transactions behind the logs outside the block windows (txcache). Incremental pickle data.pkl (not in the repository). Imports chain.py from the parent directory. Key results: Produces data.pkl = {pools: {pid: {meta: {pid,b0,tx,token,sp0,tick0,from,txindex,input}, blocks, rec, logs}}, init_scanned_to, txcache, head, end, logs_to}. 18 pools were fetched at the dry run (head about 84868280, 08:45:43 UTC).
- `/home/user/v2pons/research/session_oct9_10/dry1/replay.py`: Exact replay of the release-0.9 front rule (as at the dry run: STAKE 0.0012 ETH, TP 1%, HOLD 12, AHEAD_MAX 0.008, BOT_MIN 0.04, BOT_MINOUT 0.99, BUY_SLIP 3%, gas 103,861 buy + 103,894 sell, 135,200 sell if pulled) on data.pkl against the chain. prep(): decodes Initialize/ML/Swap logs, active liquidity via v4math, builds the engine's feed (txs with value > 0, >= 20 bytes carrying the token, to != token, excluding the launch call). engine(): the engine's copy of the price and its decisions (bot-revert check q1 < 0.99 q0, take-profit, deadline) in dry mode (virtual buy at the head of b0+2) or live mode (our buy at the tail of B). trade(): the chain side for 'fast' (B=b0+1, sell lands send+1) and 'slow' (b0+2, send+2): skip rules at the fire, our fill vs minOut, every intermediate swap re-applied by its own sqrtP shift, a bot buy under its calldata minOut (word 4) dropped as reverted, a pull at or before S = stake lost, sell priced at the head and tail of S. main(): writes replay.json, prints one line per pool and the fast/slow/dry summaries. No RPC calls (reads data.pkl only). Key results: 18 pools 08:24:57-08:44:06 UTC: fast mean +15.66% median +14.87% min +9.61% max +22.84%; slow identical to 1e-5; all exits 'take profit' at the bot's buy; dry model tp_ret mean +15.59% (first 16: +15.61%), min +9.54%, max +22.77%; max |chain ret - engine tp_ret| 0.00074; headroom (bot fill / its minOut with us ahead) 1.00903-1.00906; our tokens / our minOut 1.0308-1.0309; no bot buy dropped, no pull before the sell, head==tail pricing; eth ahead of us at the fire 0 in all 18.

## yday-protect

- `research/session_oct9_10/yday/follow.py`: Exact paper pricer of flip_engine 0.8's FOLLOW entry on every Oct 8 original-launcher launch: full-block feed replay with the engine's matching rules (value>0, calldata>=20 bytes carrying the token, to!=token, launch calls skipped), trigger >=0.0002 ETH at +7..+150, buy at T+1/T+2 priced after every other swap of its block, minOut=feed quote x0.97, TP 1% on feed buys B+1..B+2 else deadline B+2, sell lands send+1/send+2; live records from receipts; day summary under cap 3/$15 stop. Caches raw chain data in cache_follow.pkl (not in repo). Key results: 51 orig launches, 46 traded; 0.0012 land1_fast +2.19% mean, median +1.61%, worst -0.84%, 37 wins, +0.001209 ETH; land1_slow +3.28% (40 wins); land2_fast +2.70% (4 buy_reverted); land2_slow +0.84% (1 pull_first); gas 103861/103894/135200; cap-3 day = +$0.016 land1_fast at 0.0012. Writes follow.json.
- `research/session_oct9_10/yday/follow.json`: follow.py output: meta (params, gas, day_summary, conventions) + 51 launch records with per-stake (0.0012/0.004/0.012) x variant results and live records on 9 pools. Key results: ARM_LAG 6, TRIGGER_MAX 150, base fee median 20,152,000 wei; 9 live records: 8 follow (Oct 8 13:15-14:32) + 1 seat (22:48); day under engine limits: 3 trades (05:59, 06:05, 09:45) +0.000006 ETH land1_fast.
- `research/session_oct9_10/yday/follow_verified.json`: follow.json with the verifier's correction (trade 8 live: two approval txs added, ret_net -0.0973 -> -0.0990) and a meta.verification block. Key results: 552 results repriced within 5.0e-7, exits identical; report corrections: 09:45-11:14 holds 25 launches averaging +1.83% (not 27/+2.2%); three land2 reverts within 0.4% of minOut; ProtocolFeeUpdated on all 51 pools, earliest +115, all after the trade windows; 484 crowd buys behind ours checked against their own minimum: only the 06:05 0.0001 ETH dust buy reverts at 0.012.

## deploy-contracts-src

- `/home/user/v2pons/contracts/FlipRelay.sol`: The relay contract (115 lines, solc 0.8.26): buys with its own ETH and sells everything it holds straight through the Uniswap v4 PoolManager (unlock/swap/settle/take, no router, no approval); owner-only; withdraw and sweep for recovery. Key results: Functions: constructor(poolManager) sets immutables pm and owner=msg.sender; receive(); buy(key,amountIn,minOut,deadline) -> NotOwner / TooLate(block.timestamp>deadline, 0=none) / NotEthPair(currency0!=0) / Insufficient(balance<amountIn) then unlock(true,key,amountIn,minOut); sell(key,minOut,deadline) -> NotOwner / TooLate / NotEthPair / NothingToSell(balanceOf==0) then unlock(false,key,bal,minOut); unlockCallback -> NotPoolManager, one exact-input swap with price limit MIN_SQRT_PRICE+1 (buy) or MAX_SQRT_PRICE-1 (sell), BalanceDelta split by sar(128)/signextend(15), sign-sanity revert TooLittleOut(0,minOut), TooLittleOut(out,minOut) if out<minOut; buy: settle{value:-d0}, take(token) if out>0; sell: if owed>0 sync+transfer(0xa9059cbb)+settle (TransferFailed), take(ETH) if out>0; withdraw(amount, 0=all) require 'withdraw'; sweep(token) require 'sweep'. After a FULL liquidity pull with minOut 0 (what the engine sends) the sell does not revert by logic: out=0, transfer and take skipped, returns 0, tokens stay in the relay; a partial pull sells everything into what is left at any price. Observed on chain (HANDOFF 16.1): the sell reverted after 335,520/350,000 gas = out-of-gas walking the empty tick bitmap, so with SELL_GAS 350,000 the practical outcome is a reverted sell and tokens left in the relay.
- `/home/user/v2pons/contracts/FlipRelay.json`: compile.py's artifact: abi (17 entries), creation and runtime bytecode, immutableReferences, selectors, source_sha256. Key results: solc 0.8.26, optimizer_runs 1000, evm cancun; runtime 5,219 bytes, creation 5,470 bytes; source_sha256 14616699fe7c1279f6398c9da154be3f9890f1f05d9f2101736052f28907fd60 matches the current FlipRelay.sol (verified); selectors buy 5ed06f60, sell 137f1fdd, owner 8da5cb5b, pm 8970cdff, sweep 01681a62, unlockCallback 91dd7346, withdraw 2e1a7d4d; immutable AST id 71 (pm) 11 references, 73 (owner) 7 references, each 32 bytes.
- `/home/user/v2pons/contracts/RoundTripHarness.sol`: Test-only contract (never deployed) placed by eth_call state override as the relay's owner: runs one buy and one sell through the relay and checks the ETH accounting and that no tokens are left. Key results: run(relay,key,amountIn,minOutBuy) returns (tokensOut, tokensHeld, ethBack, ethHeldAfter); requires eth0-amountIn+ethBack==ethHeldAfter ('eth accounting') and balanceOf(relay)==0 ('tokens left'). Compiled by relay_sim.py with optimizer 200 runs (not 1000).
- `/home/user/v2pons/contracts/compile.py`: python3 contracts/compile.py: compiles FlipRelay.sol with py-solc-x 0.8.26 (installs it if missing) into FlipRelay.json. Key results: Settings optimizer enabled 1000 runs, evmVersion cancun; writes abi, bytecode, deployedBytecode, immutableReferences, selectors, source_sha256; prints the runtime byte count and selectors. Needs network to download solc once.
- `/home/user/v2pons/deploy/flip_setup.sh`: sudo bash deploy/flip_setup.sh: idempotent box install: venv /opt/flip-venv (websockets rlp eth-account eth-utils coincurve py-solc-x), /etc/flip (700) and engine.env (600, written only if absent, DRY_RUN=1 template of release 0.1), /var/log/flip, logrotate, the flip-engine unit; restarts the service and prints the last start line. Key results: Unit: After/Wants network-online.target; EnvironmentFile=/etc/flip/engine.env; WorkingDirectory=$REPO (absolute, resolved at run time); ExecStart=/opt/flip-venv/bin/python3 $REPO/src/flip_engine.py; Restart=always; RestartSec=3; Nice=-5; WantedBy=multi-user.target. NO TimeoutStopSec, NO KillMode, NO KillSignal: systemd defaults apply (DefaultTimeoutStopSec 90 s on Ubuntu, KillMode=control-group, SIGTERM then SIGKILL). Logrotate: daily rotate 30 missingok notifempty compress delaycompress copytruncate. Env template keys: DRY_RUN LOG_PATH FEED_URL SEQ_URL RPC_URL LAUNCHERS POOL_MANAGER WALLET RELAY PRIVATE_KEY STAKE_ETH 0.0012 ETH_USD 2570 MIN_TRIGGER_ETH 0.0002 FEE_MIN_PPM 0 FEE_MAX_PPM 9999 MIN_LIQUIDITY 1e21 TRIGGER_MAX_BLOCKS 150 BUY_SLIP 0.03 BUY_DEADLINE_S 3 HOLD_BLOCKS 4 SELL_LEAD_BLOCKS 2 SELL_FALLBACK_S 0.5 TAKE_PROFIT 0.01 MAX_TRADES_PER_DAY 10 DAILY_STOP_USD 15 (no FRONT_* keys: code defaults). Every run pip-installs (network) and ends with an unconditional systemctl restart + sleep 6.
- `/home/user/v2pons/deploy/relay_ops.py`: sudo /opt/flip-venv/bin/python3 deploy/relay_ops.py status | deposit <eth> | withdraw [eth] | sweep 0xTOKEN | payout 0xADDR <eth> [--yes]: the relay's money from the box. Key results: Reads /etc/flip/engine.env keys RPC_URL, WALLET, RELAY, PRIVATE_KEY, STAKE_ETH, LOG_PATH. status (no key needed): wallet balance and pending nonce, relay balance and owner() (== wallet check), today's live trades and P&L from the UNROTATED log only, service active/stopped. Every other command sys.exits if systemctl is-active flip-engine, and if Account(PRIVATE_KEY) != WALLET or RELAY empty. deposit: plain value tx to the relay (receive()). withdraw: data 0x2e1a7d4d+word(amount) (no amount -> 0 -> everything). sweep: 0x01681a62+word(token). payout: 2 args required, is_address + checksum check for mixed case, refuses our own wallet/relay, amount>0, amt+21000*gp*2+GAS_RESERVE_WEI(0.003 ETH) <= balance, prints contract/plain destination, typed 'yes' unless --yes. send(): nonce 'pending', gasPrice 2x eth_gasPrice, gas 1.3x estimate, chainId 4663, legacy tx, polls the receipt for 60 s and prints the hash with ok/status (no retry; after 60 s prints 'status None' though the tx may still land).
- `/home/user/v2pons/deploy/relay_deploy.py`: sudo /opt/flip-venv/bin/python3 deploy/relay_deploy.py [--yes] [--write-env]: deploys FlipRelay from the flip wallet and verifies it. Key results: Refuses while the service is active; reads PRIVATE_KEY, WALLET, RPC_URL from /etc/flip/engine.env; checks the artifact's source_sha256 against FlipRelay.sol; data = bytecode + word(PM 0x8366a39c…); prints balance, gas estimate and max cost, exits if balance < gas*1.3*gp*2; asks 'yes'; legacy tx gasPrice 2x, gas 1.3x, chainId 4663; waits 120 s for the receipt (exit if none or reverted); checks code == relay_runtime(wallet) byte for byte, owner()==wallet, pm()==PM (exit 1 on any mismatch); simulates buy 1e14 wei via eth_call with a 1e16 balance override on the operator's latest unpulled pool and compares to v4math.buy_out_int (ok if |rel|<1e-9, else 'MISMATCH: do not use the relay'); --write-env rewrites RELAY= atomically (tmp + chmod 600 + replace). Failure mode: latest_operator_pool()/pool_state() raise SystemExit, which the 'except Exception' does not catch, so with no unpulled operator pool the script exits before --write-env writes RELAY= (the address was printed above).
- `/home/user/v2pons/deploy/relay_sim.py`: python3 deploy/relay_sim.py [poolId] [eth]: a buy and a sell through the relay on a live pool with no transaction (eth_call state overrides); also the shared helpers rpc(), word(), relay_runtime(), pool_state(), latest_operator_pool() imported by relay_ops.py and relay_deploy.py. Key results: Constants PM 0x8366a39cc670b4001a1121b8f6a443a643e40951, LAUNCHER 0x3194e32622c5d860a0572c74edda99dcfd8fc827, RELAY_AT 0xf1…f1, HARNESS_AT 0xa1…a1; RPC from env RPC_URL or the public endpoint; rpc() retries 6 times on 429/network with 2s*(i+1) backoff. pool_state scans Initialize logs back 2M blocks in 200k chunks, sums adds with tickLower<tick0<=tickUpper, takes the last Swap's sqrtP/liquidity. latest_operator_pool: Initialize with currency0=0 in the last 400k blocks, hooks 0, tickSpacing 200, tx to the launcher, no negative ModifyLiquidity. Default amount 1e14 wei (0.0001 ETH); relay balance override 2x amount; compiles RoundTripHarness with solcx (downloads solc 0.8.26 once). Prints tokens out vs v4math ('exact' or the relative error), ETH back and round-trip %, 'accounting ok'; then re-runs with minOutBuy = tokens_out+1 and prints 'TooLittleOut ok' if 'd06c7f4b' or 'revert' is in the error: d06c7f4b is NOT TooLittleOut(uint256,uint256) (e20a4c5d), so the check passes on any revert. Dead code: an empty for-loop in relay_runtime and 'gas = … if False else None'.
- `/home/user/v2pons/deploy/results.py`: sudo python3 deploy/results.py [hours=24]: the results screen (stdlib only; needs root for the env file and systemctl for the service state; one RPC call per balance, 5 s timeout). Key results: Reads /var/log/flip/engine.jsonl and every rotation (.N, .N.gz); keeps the latest start and flow lines whenever they were; every live trade_done in all logs deduped by (t, nonce). Header: service RUNNING/STOPPED, release, LIVE/DRY RUN (from the start line's dry_run, else the env), entry, 'max N trades a day' = follow's max_trades_per_day; halted from /var/log/flip/HALTED or the flow line's halted (HALTED in memory); follow strategy HALTED from HALTED.follow (HEAD only, 792a8c5); front line: ON, DRY RUN/LIVE, stake front_stake_eth, 'max front_max_per_day a day', 'its own daily stop $front_daily_stop_usd', HALTED from HALTED.front or 'OFF for this run' from flow.front_halted. PROFIT BY STRATEGY: columns last hour / today (UTC midnight) / last H h / since first trade; $ labels stake*ETH_USD ($3 follow, $15 front); per strategy % up, $ a trade, mean and worst ret, 'pull after our sell: g blocks xN … none within 30 blocks xM; BEFORE our sell xK <== halts (closest g)', 'by stake' when more than one stake (stake field in wei). Then launches seen / tradable / per entry (ENTRY=auto), new fingerprints, 'not traded:' skip reasons over the whole window (reason text 'daily cap' is shared by both strategies), LIVE TRADES (fire matched to trade_done by nonce in [fire, next fire), REFUSED/in progress/NO RESULT), front: last 5 rows + FRONT LIVE summary, FRONT DRY RUN summary, DRY-RUN DECISIONS, ALARMS (last 5), money line. The owner's ~/results.py (4e8d350) lacks the 4 follow-halt lines; otherwise identical.

- `/home/user/v2pons/src/v4math.py`: Exact single-range Uniswap v4 pricing: float buy_out/sell_out/sqrt_after_buy for scoring, integer buy_out_int/sell_out_int reproducing the PoolManager's rounding for minOut, active_liquidity(tick0, adds). Key results: Docstring: validated on 33,769 real buys (median relative error 6e-15) and 26,720 real sells (2e-5; 1e-3 cases are two-range pools). buy_out_int: fee taken on input rounded up ((x*fee+999999)//1e6), sqrt_new rounded up, output rounded down; sell_out_int: sqrt_new rounded down, ETH out rounded down. active_liquidity sums positive deltas with lo < tick0 <= hi. Imported by flip_engine, score, relay_sim, relay_deploy and most research scripts.

Constants: SQRT_P 1292187213925453985058262482146976 (a real operator pool's initial sqrtPriceX96), LIQ 6.131e22; fees 200/1134/20000 ppm; integer results within max(2, 1e-15 rel) of exact and rounded down; floats within 1e-6; round trip loses < 2.5x fee + 1e-6.

- `/home/user/v2pons/deploy/results.py`: The owner's one-screen report (290 lines, 4 lines added since 3c3f696 for HALTED.follow): reads /var/log/flip/engine.jsonl and rotations, /etc/flip/engine.env, the halt files, systemctl, and the RPC for balances. Key results: Reads events: start and flow (header/status; latest by t), trade_done non-dry (dedup by (t, nonce); fields t, nonce, entry, pnl_usd, ret, sell_after_buy_blocks, hold_blocks, pull_after_buy_blocks, pulled_before_sell, pull_check, buy.status, fee, sell_why, stake, dry, pid), armed (eligible, entry, fingerprint, t), skip (why truncated to 60 chars), fire (t, nonce, pid, fee, entry), buy_rejected (pid, reply), would_fire (pid, entry), would_sell (pid, why, tp_ret, after_buy), alarm (what). Does not read: feed_connected, feed_error, feed_stall, launch, launch_undecoded, launch_no_receipt, launch_no_pool, first_buy, buy_reply, buy_seen, sell_sent, nonce, no_first_buy, front_bot_reverts, stopping, stopped; nor trade_done.near_miss (it reads HALTED.follow instead). Header 'max N trades a day' = follow's MAX_TRADES_PER_DAY (start.max_trades_per_day, else env); 'front strategy: ... max N a day' = start.front_max_per_day else env FRONT_MAX_PER_DAY else '?'. 'not traded:' aggregates skip whys over the whole window (24 h), so 'daily cap x60' cannot distinguish follow's cap from front's.
